```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function height sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a) ** 2)

by working with the scale-invariant normalization sum(a)=1.  The optimizer
combines several complementary ideas:

  * FFT-based exact score and active-set gradients,
  * smoothed L-infinity descent for the self-convolution,
  * an active-set cross-subproblem that finds a direction b which reduces
    the first-order growth of the current max convolution coefficients,
  * optional exact active-set LP directions for late-stage polishing,
  * multiplicative deconvolution-style flattening moves,
  * symmetrization trials and smooth restarts,
  * multi-length search with final exact validation when time permits.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_prob(a):
    """Normalize a finite nonnegative vector to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def symmetrize(a):
    """Average a sequence with its reversal."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    return 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])


def resample_probability(a, n_new):
    """Smoothly interpolate a probability mass vector to a new length."""
    a = normalize_prob(a)
    n_old = a.size
    if n_new <= 0:
        n_new = 1
    if n_new == n_old:
        return a.copy()
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_new)
    return b / s


def log_from_prob(a):
    """Stable log of a positive probability vector."""
    a = normalize_prob(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    w = np.log(a)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    maxw = float(np.max(w))
    w = np.maximum(w, maxw - 50.0)
    w = w - maxw
    return w


def prob_from_log(w):
    """Stable softmax with a log-floor to avoid exact zeros."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, -100.0)
    maxw = float(np.max(w))
    w = np.maximum(w, maxw - 45.0)
    w = w - maxw
    e = np.exp(w)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    return e / s


def fft_score_info(a, N, n):
    """Return DFT, full linear convolution, max coefficient, and score."""
    a = normalize_prob(a)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(c))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        c = c / s
    mx = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * mx)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = float("inf")
    return A, c, mx, score


def fft_score_only(a, N, n):
    """FFT-based objective value for a probability vector."""
    return fft_score_info(a, N, n)[3]


def quick_score(a):
    """Self-contained score for an arbitrary vector."""
    a = normalize_prob(a)
    n = a.size
    N = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    return fft_score_only(a, N, n)


def soft_top_weights(c, mx, beta=100.0, uni_mix=0.15):
    """Smooth top-coefficient weights with a small uniform floor."""
    L = c.size
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, L))
    x = (c - mx) / mx
    x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(beta * x, -60.0, 0.0)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = p / s
    if uni_mix > 0.0:
        p = (1.0 - uni_mix) * p + (uni_mix / float(max(1, L)))
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            p = p / s
    return p


def adam_delta(grad, m, v, t, lr, max_step=1.0):
    """One Adam update and the resulting step."""
    grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    t_new = int(t) + 1
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    m_new = m * beta1 + (1.0 - beta1) * grad
    v_new = v * beta2 + (1.0 - beta2) * (grad * grad)
    mhat = m_new / (1.0 - beta1 ** t_new)
    vhat = v_new / (1.0 - beta2 ** t_new)
    delta = lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-12)
    delta = np.where(np.isfinite(delta), delta, 0.0)
    delta = np.clip(delta, -max_step, max_step)
    return delta, m_new, v_new, t_new


def cross_active_inner(a, A, U, c, b, w_b, m_b, v_b, t_b, lr, beta,
                       inner, active_tol, kmax, mix_y, N, n):
    """
    For fixed a, inner-optimize b for the smooth active-set problem:
        minimize max_{k active} ((a*b)_k - c_k)
    subject to b >= 0, sum(b) = 1.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return b, w_b, m_b, v_b, t_b, 0.0

    thr = mx * (1.0 - active_tol)
    idx = np.where(c >= thr)[0]
    amax = int(np.argmax(c))
    if amax not in idx:
        idx = np.concatenate((idx, np.array([amax], dtype=int)))

    if idx.size > kmax:
        kk = int(kmax)
        vals = c[idx]
        part = np.argpartition(vals, -kk)[-kk:]
        idx = idx[part]
    else:
        if idx.size < 4:
            kk = min(4, L)
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -kk)[-kk:]

    base = c[idx]
    last_maxpay = 0.0
    P = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

    for _ in range(int(inner)):
        B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:L]
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)

        pay = h[idx] - base
        maxpay = float(np.max(pay)) if pay.size else 0.0
        last_maxpay = maxpay

        if mx > 0.0:
            z = (pay - maxpay) / mx
        else:
            z = np.zeros(pay.size, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        z = np.clip(beta * z, -60.0, 0.0)

        y = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(y))
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            y = y / s
        else:
            y = np.ones(pay.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, pay.size))

        if mix_y > 0.0 and y.size:
            y = (1.0 - mix_y) * y + (mix_y / float(y.size))
            s = float(np.sum(y))
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                y = y / s

        P.fill(0.0)
        P[idx] = y
        Pfft = np.fft.rfft(P, N)
        G = np.fft.irfft(Pfft * U, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)

        gw = b * (G - float(np.dot(b, G)))
        delta, m_b, v_b, t_b = adam_delta(gw, m_b, v_b, t_b, lr, 0.5)
        w_b = w_b - delta
        b = prob_from_log(w_b)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            b = a.copy()
            w_b = log_from_prob(a)
            break

    return b, w_b, m_b, v_b, t_b, last_maxpay


def self_gradient_step(a, A, U, c, mx, w_a, m_a, v_a, t_a, lr, beta):
    """One smoothed-Linf Adam step for the self-convolution objective."""
    N = A.size * 2 - 1 if A.size else 1
    n = a.size
    p = soft_top_weights(c, mx, beta=beta, uni_mix=0.15)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    G = 2.0 * np.fft.irfft(P * U, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)

    gw = a * (G - float(np.dot(a, G)))
    delta, m_a, v_a, t_a = adam_delta(gw, m_a, v_a, t_a, lr, 0.5)
    w_a = w_a - delta
    a_new = prob_from_log(w_a)
    return a_new, w_a, m_a, v_a, t_a


def make_clipped_target(y, m_cap):
    """Build a target with desired cap and unit sum."""
    L = y.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if m_cap <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m_cap):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    t = np.minimum(y, m_cap)
    t = np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(t))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    if s < 1.0:
        deficit = 1.0 - s
        room = np.maximum(m_cap - t, 0.0)
        cap = float(np.sum(room))
        if cap > deficit:
            t = t + (deficit / cap) * room
        else:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    s = float(np.sum(t))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    t = t / s
    return np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 0.0)


def deconv_self_candidate(a, N, phase, iters, mix, target_type, target_m, n):
    """Multiplicative deconvolution-style flattening heuristic."""
    b = a.copy()
    L = 2 * n - 1
    karr = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64)
    center = 0.5 * (L - 1)
    if center > 0.0:
        xw = (karr - center) / center
        win_shape = np.maximum(1.0 - 0.6 * xw * xw, 0.02)
        win_shape = win_shape / float(np.sum(win_shape))
    else:
        win_shape = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, L))

    for _ in range(int(iters)):
        B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
        y = np.fft.irfft(B * B, N)[:L]
        y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
        y = np.maximum(y, 1e-300)

        if target_type == 0:
            t = 1.0 / float(L)
        elif target_type == 1:
            t = make_clipped_target(y, target_m)
        elif target_type == 2:
            t = win_shape
        else:
            t = 1.0 / float(L)

        r = t / y
        mr = float(np.mean(r))
        if np.isfinite(mr) and mr > 0.0:
            r = r / mr
        r = np.where(np.isfinite(r), r, 0.0)

        P = np.fft.rfft(r, N)
        U = phase * np.conjugate(B)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(P * U, N)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        corr = np.where(np.isfinite(corr), corr, 0.0)
        corr = np.maximum(corr, 0.0)

        b_new = b * corr
        s = float(np.sum(b_new))
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            b_new = b_new / s
        else:
            b_new = b.copy()

        if mix < 1.0:
            b_new = (1.0 - mix) * b + mix * b_new
        b = normalize_prob(b_new)

    return b


def find_best_mixin(a, b, base_score, N, n, trial_ts):
    """Try convex mixtures of a and b, also considering symmetrization."""
    best_a = None
    best_score = base_score

    for tval in trial_ts:
        if tval <= 0.0:
            cand = a.copy()
        elif tval >= 1.0:
            cand = b.copy()
        else:
            cand = (1.0 - tval) * a + tval * b
        cand = normalize_prob(cand)

        sc = fft_score_only(cand, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = cand

        cand2 = symmetrize(cand)
        cand2 = normalize_prob(cand2)
        sc2 = fft_score_only(cand2, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = cand2

    return best_a, best_score


def smooth_log_noise(rng, w, sigma):
    """Add smooth low-frequency plus genuinely random log perturbations."""
    n = w.size
    out = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if out.size == 0:
        return out
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(1, 6):
        amp = sigma * abs(float(rng.normal())) / float(r + 1)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        out = out + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * r * x + ph)
    out = out + sigma * 0.15 * np.asarray(rng.normal(size=n), dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = out - float(np.max(out))
    return out


def make_cosine_seq(n, a1=0.0, a2=0.0, a3=0.0):
    """A smooth symmetric nonnegative sequence."""
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - 0.5 * (n - 1)) / float(n - 1)
    base = (1.0
            + float(a1) * np.cos(np.pi * t)
            + float(a2) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
            + float(a3) * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * t))
    base = np.where(np.isfinite(base), base, 0.0)
    base = np.maximum(base, 1e-3)
    return normalize_prob(base)


def cosine_grid():
    """A small grid of useful cosine initial shapes."""
    return [
        (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
        (-0.70, 0.0, 0.0),
        (-0.45, 0.0, 0.0),
        (-0.80, 0.10, 0.0),
        (-0.35, 0.25, 0.0),
        (-0.55, 0.0, 0.20),
        (0.30, 0.0, 0.0),
        (0.60, 0.0, 0.0),
        (-0.20, 0.55, -0.20),
        (-0.40, 0.30, 0.25),
        (0.0, 0.70, -0.35),
    ]


def load_initial_sequence():
    """Load the previous/global construction if present."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 8:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr / s


def exact_score_array(a):
    """Exact objective using np.convolve."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    val = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")


def add_candidate(top, a, score, max_items=16):
    """Maintain a small list of best FFT-scored candidates."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return top
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return top
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return top

    if len(top) < max_items:
        top.append((float(score), a.copy()))
        return top

    worst = 0
    worst_score = float(top[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(top)):
        si = float(top[i][0])
        if si > worst_score:
            worst_score = si
            worst = i
    if float(score) < worst_score:
        top[worst] = (float(score), a.copy())
    return top


def get_sorted_top(top):
    """Sort a candidate list by score without lambda functions."""
    top = list(top)
    m = len(top)
    for i in range(m):
        minj = i
        minv = float(top[minj][0])
        for j in range(i + 1, m):
            sv = float(top[j][0])
            if sv < minv:
                minv = sv
                minj = j
        if minj != i:
            top[i], top[minj] = top[minj], top[i]
    return top


def choose_large_length(n0):
    """Choose a longer search length when available."""
    if n0 <= 4096:
        return 8192
    if n0 <= 8192:
        return 16384
    if n0 <= 12288:
        return 16384
    return n0


def solve_active_lp(a, c, idx, n):
    """
    Solve the active-set linear program:
        min t
        s.t. (a*b)_k - t <= c_k for k in idx
             sum(b)=1, b>=0
    Return b and optimal t if successful.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None, None
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=int)
    K = idx.size
    if K == 0 or n > 12000:
        return None, None

    A = np.zeros((K, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    for i in range(K):
        k = int(idx[i])
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi >= lo:
            js = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=int)
            A[i, lo:hi + 1] = a[k - js]
        A[i, n] = -1.0
        b_ub[i] = c[k]

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
    beq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    bounds.append((-1.0, 1.0))

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=Aeq,
            b_eq=beq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": 20}
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, None

    if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False):
        b = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        tval = float(res.x[n])
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(b))
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            b = b / s
            return b, tval

    return None, None


def optimize_candidate(a_init, budget_s=300.0, target_score=1.5027, seed=1, name=""):
    """
    Local/global search from one initial sequence.
    Returns (best_array, best_score, top_candidate_list).
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    a = normalize_prob(a_init)
    if a.size < 64:
        a = make_cosine_seq(4096, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
    if a.size > 20000:
        a = resample_probability(a, 16384)

    n = a.size
    N = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(N // 2 + 1) * (n - 1) / float(N))

    cur = a.copy()
    A, c, mx, cur_score = fft_score_info(cur, N, n)

    best = cur.copy()
    best_score = cur_score
    top = add_candidate([], best, best_score)

    w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
    m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t_a = 0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    lr_cross = 0.20
    lr_self = 0.12
    base_lr = 0.15

    stall = 0
    outer = 0
    last_print = start
    last_lp = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        A, c, mx, cur_score = fft_score_info(cur, N, n)
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_score = best_score
            w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
            m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t_a = 0
            continue

        U = phase * np.conjugate(A)

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = cur_score
            best = cur.copy()
            top = add_candidate(top, best, best_score)
            stall = 0

        # Periodic symmetrization trial.
        if outer % 10 == 0:
            cur_s = symmetrize(cur)
            cur_s = normalize_prob(cur_s)
            sc_s = fft_score_only(cur_s, N, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_s) and sc_s < cur_score - 1e-13:
                cur = cur_s
                cur_score = sc_s
                w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
                m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                t_a = 0
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best = cur.copy()
                    top = add_candidate(top, best, best_score)
                stall = 0
                outer += 1
                continue

        # Adaptive stage parameters.
        if cur_score > 1.70:
            active_tol = 0.015
            kmax = 16
            inner = 8
            beta_cross = 60.0
            beta_self = 80.0
            base_lr = 0.25
        elif cur_score > 1.58:
            active_tol = 0.007
            kmax = 32
            inner = 10
            beta_cross = 150.0
            beta_self = 200.0
            base_lr = 0.15
        elif cur_score > 1.505:
            active_tol = 0.0025
            kmax = 48
            inner = 14
            beta_cross = 400.0
            beta_self = 500.0
            base_lr = 0.08
        else:
            active_tol = 0.001
            kmax = 80
            inner = 18
            beta_cross = 800.0
            beta_self = 1000.0
            base_lr = 0.04

        if lr_cross < 0.5 * base_lr:
            lr_cross = base_lr
        elif lr_cross > 1.5 * base_lr:
            lr_cross = base_lr

        improved = False
        now = time.monotonic()

        # Late-stage exact linear-program directions.
        if (linprog is not None
                and cur_score < 1.62
                and n <= 9000
                and now - last_lp > 45.0
                and (stall > 20 or outer % 40 == 0)):
            kk = min(24, c.size)
            if kk >= c.size:
                lp_idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=int)
            else:
                lp_idx = np.argpartition(c, -kk)[-kk:]
            b_lp, lp_t = solve_active_lp(cur, c, lp_idx, n)
            last_lp = now
            if b_lp is not None and lp_t < -1e-12:
                cand, sc = find_best_mixin(cur, b_lp, cur_score, N, n, (0.25, 0.5, 1.0))
                if cand is not None:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
                    m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t_a = 0
                    improved = True
                    lr_cross = min(0.5, lr_cross * 1.05)

        if not improved:
            if outer % 30 == 25:
                move = 2
            elif outer % 3 == 2:
                move = 1
            else:
                move = 0

            if move == 0:
                b_init = cur.copy()
                w_b = log_from_prob(b_init)
                m_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v_b = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                t_b = 0
                b_new, _, _, _, _, _ = cross_active_inner(
                    cur, A, U, c, b_init, w_b, m_b, v_b, t_b,
                    lr_cross, beta_cross, inner, active_tol, kmax, 0.5, N, n
                )
                cand, sc = find_best_mixin(cur, b_new, cur_score, N, n, (0.25, 0.5, 1.0))
                if cand is not None:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
                    m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t_a = 0
                    improved = True
                    lr_cross = min(0.5, lr_cross * 1.05)
                else:
                    lr_cross = max(0.4 * base_lr, lr_cross * 0.90)

            elif move == 1:
                a_new, w_new, m_a_new, v_a_new, t_a_new = self_gradient_step(
                    cur, A, U, c, mx, w_cur, m_a, v_a, t_a, lr_self, beta_self
                )
                m_a, v_a, t_a = m_a_new, v_a_new, t_a_new

                cand, sc = find_best_mixin(cur, a_new, cur_score, N, n, (1.0,))
                if cand is not None:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
                    improved = True
                    lr_self = min(0.35, lr_self * 1.05)
                else:
                    lr_self = max(0.4 * base_lr, lr_self * 0.90)

            else:
                target_m = max(float(target_score) / float(2.0 * n), 0.85 * mx)
                shape = deconv_self_candidate(
                    cur, N, phase,
                    iters=20,
                    mix=0.35,
                    target_type=(outer % 2),
                    target_m=target_m,
                    n=n
                )
                cand, sc = find_best_mixin(cur, shape, cur_score, N, n, (0.5, 1.0))
                if cand is not None:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
                    m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    t_a = 0
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = cur_score
                best = cur.copy()
                top = add_candidate(top, best, best_score)
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 120 or cur_score > best_score + 0.05:
                # Smooth random restart from a retained candidate.
                base_choice = int(rng.integers(0, len(top)))
                base_w = log_from_prob(top[base_choice][1])
                sigma = 0.25 if cur_score < 1.6 else 0.65
                w_try = smooth_log_noise(rng, base_w, sigma)
                a_try = prob_from_log(w_try)
                a_sym = symmetrize(a_try)
                a_sym = normalize_prob(a_sym)

                sc1 = fft_score_only(a_try, N, n)
                sc2 = fft_score_only(a_sym, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and (not np.isfinite(sc1) or sc2 < sc1):
                    cur = a_sym
                    cur_score = sc2
                else:
                    cur = a_try
                    cur_score = sc1

                if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
                    cur = best.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score

                w_cur = log_from_prob(cur)
                m_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v_a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                t_a = 0
                lr_cross = base_lr
                lr_self = 0.12
                stall = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 45.0:
            print(f"{name}: n={n} best={best_score:.6f} cur={cur_score:.6f} "
                  f"stall={stall} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.0f}s")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        outer += 1

    # Final symmetrization trial on the best.
    best_s = symmetrize(best)
    best_s = normalize_prob(best_s)
    sc_s = fft_score_only(best_s, N, n)
    if np.isfinite(sc_s) and sc_s < best_score - 1e-13:
        best = best_s
        best_score = sc_s

    top = add_candidate(top, best, best_score)
    return best, best_score, top


def merge_top(global_top, other_top):
    """Merge another candidate list into the global top list."""
    for item in other_top:
        try:
            sc = float(item[0])
            arr = item[1]
        except Exception:
            continue
        global_top = add_candidate(global_top, arr, sc)
    return global_top


def build_initial_candidates(parent, seed=123):
    """Build and lightly score a diverse initial candidate pool."""
    top = []
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if parent is None:
        for n in (4096, 8192):
            a = make_cosine_seq(n, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
            top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))
            for params in cosine_grid():
                a = make_cosine_seq(n, params[0], params[1], params[2])
                top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))
            for _ in range(3):
                w = np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, 0.9, size=n), dtype=np.float64)
                w = smooth_log_noise(rng, w, 0.5)
                a = prob_from_log(w)
                top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))
                a = symmetrize(a)
                top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))
        return top

    p = normalize_prob(parent)
    if p.size > 20000:
        p = resample_probability(p, 16384)

    n_small = p.size
    top = add_candidate(top, p, quick_score(p))
    ps = symmetrize(p)
    ps = normalize_prob(ps)
    top = add_candidate(top, ps, quick_score(ps))

    n_large = choose_large_length(n_small)
    if n_large != n_small:
        up = resample_probability(p, n_large)
        top = add_candidate(top, up, quick_score(up))
        up = symmetrize(up)
        up = normalize_prob(up)
        top = add_candidate(top, up, quick_score(up))

    # Add shaped starts in the small length, and their upsampled large versions.
    for params in cosine_grid():
        a = make_cosine_seq(n_small, params[0], params[1], params[2])
        top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))
        if n_large != n_small:
            b = resample_probability(a, n_large)
            top = add_candidate(top, b, quick_score(b))

    # A few random smooth starts.
    for n_try in (n_small, n_large) if n_large != n_small else (n_small,):
        if n_try is None:
            continue
        for _ in range(2):
            w = np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, 0.9, size=int(n_try)), dtype=np.float64)
            w = smooth_log_noise(rng, w, 0.55)
            a = prob_from_log(w)
            top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))
            a = symmetrize(a)
            top = add_candidate(top, a, quick_score(a))

    return top


def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a valid list of non-negative finite floats, sum=1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        a = a * (0.01 / max(s2, 1e-300))
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.ones(max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, a.size))

    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point required by the harness.
    Runs the multi-method FFT search and returns a valid final sequence.
    """
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0
    total_budget = min(max(total_budget, 10.0), 2395.0)

    start_total = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start_total + total_budget
    search_budget = max(30.0, total_budget - 90.0)
    search_deadline = start_total + search_budget

    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    global_top = build_initial_candidates(parent, seed=int(seed) + 17)

    if not global_top:
        a = make_cosine_seq(8192, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        global_top = add_candidate([], a, quick_score(a))

    sorted_top = get_sorted_top(global_top)

    primary = sorted_top[0][1]
    primary_len = int(primary.size)

    alt = None
    for item in sorted_top:
        try:
            if int(item[1].size) != primary_len:
                alt = item[1]
                break
        except Exception:
            continue

    # Stage 1: best initial candidate.
    remaining1 = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining1 > 30.0:
        budget1 = min(remaining1, int(0.45 * search_budget))
        if alt is None or search_budget <= 600:
            budget1 = remaining1
        best1, score1, top1 = optimize_candidate(
            primary, budget_s=budget1,
            target_score=1.5027,
            seed=int(seed) + 1,
            name="stage1"
        )
        global_top = merge_top(global_top, top1)

    # Stage 2: a different length, seeded by the current best if possible.
    if alt is not None:
        alt_len = int(alt.size)
        remaining2 = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining2 > 60.0:
            budget2 = min(remaining2, int(0.45 * search_budget))
            start_alt = resample_probability(best1, alt_len)
            start_alt_s = symmetrize(start_alt)
            start_alt_s = normalize_prob(start_alt_s)

            s_alt0 = quick_score(alt)
            s_alt1 = quick_score(start_alt)
            s_alt2 = quick_score(start_alt_s)
            if s_alt1 <= s_alt0 and s_alt1 <= s_alt2:
                start_alt = start_alt
            elif s_alt2 <= s_alt0:
                start_alt = start_alt_s
            else:
                start_alt = alt

            best2, score2, top2 = optimize_candidate(
                start_alt, budget_s=budget2,
                target_score=1.5027,
                seed=int(seed) + 2,
                name="stage2"
            )
            global_top = merge_top(global_top, top2)

    # Stage 3: final polish of the best retained candidate.
    remaining3 = search_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining3 > 30.0:
        sorted_now = get_sorted_top(global_top)
        final_start = sorted_now[0][1]
        best3, score3, top3 = optimize_candidate(
            final_start, budget_s=remaining3,
            target_score=1.5027,
            seed=int(seed) + 3,
            name="polish"
        )
        global_top = merge_top(global_top, top3)
        # Directly ensure this best is in global_top.
        global_top = add_candidate(global_top, best3, score3)

    sorted_final_top = get_sorted_top(global_top)
    if not sorted_final_top:
        return finalize_sequence(make_cosine_seq(4096, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0))

    # Exact validation of the strongest candidates, when time and size permit.
    best_final = sorted_final_top[0][1]
    best_final_score = float("inf")

    for item in sorted_final_top[:10]:
        try:
            a = item[1]
            n = int(a.size)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 4.0:
            break
        # Be conservative with expensive exact convolutions.
        if n > 9000 and (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) < 120.0:
            continue
        try:
            val = exact_score_array(a)
        except Exception:
            val = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_final_score:
            best_final_score = val
            best_final = a.copy()
            print(f"final exact candidate: n={n} exact={val:.6f}")

    if best_final is None:
        best_final = sorted_final_top[0][1]
    else:
        # Try exact symmetrized version of the selected final candidate.
        try:
            bs = symmetrize(normalize_prob(best_final))
            if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 6.0:
                if int(bs.size) <= 9000 or (hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 120.0:
                    valb = exact_score_array(bs)
                    if np.isfinite(valb) and valb < best_final_score:
                        best_final = bs
                        best_final_score = valb
                        print(f"final exact sym improved: exact={valb:.6f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    if "best_final_score" in locals():
        print(f"propose_candidate done: best evaluated score={best_final_score:.6f}, "
              f"length={int(best_final.size)}")

    return finalize_sequence(best_final)
```
